Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a machine_learning algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 194. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 579/2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on machine_learning structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 194. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 579. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The machine_learning operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 179. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 400 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 179 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the machine_learning structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 400. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
